# Building reference sheets

Coordinates, dimensions and orientation come from the georeferenced campus footprint. Bearings describe the longest mapped edge (0–180° from north), not a photographed facade. Width/depth are east–west/north–south bounds. No photograph was used to infer horizontal scale.

## LASU International Library · arcgis:University_Property:3

- Treatment: detailed; confidence: observed.
- Footprint: 110.763 × 99.753 m bounds; 7410.066 m²; longest edge 77.768°; 1 parts; 1 courtyards.
- Source retrieved: 2026-09-09T21:47:28.268397+00:00 (survey date unknown).
